Sep 2024): Character portraits and campaign moments**\n- More narrative prompts telling stories: battles, revelations, emotional beats\n- Added `+ character portrait`, `+ cinematic`, `+ action shot` modifiers\n- Introduced chibi zodiac animal series (doe, stag, owl, bat, fox, snake, mongoose, pangolin, turtle, wolverine, wolf, hare, crab, bull, capricorn -- 100+ variants)\n- Town shopkeeper portraits for a murder mystery arc (Sasha, Flo, Brock, Ginger, Bart, Selena, Quill, Piper, Dusty, Rusty, Lacey, William Whiskers, Khem, Selene)\n\n**Phase 3 (Mar 2026): Webcomic/motion webtoon production**\n- Completely different approach: cinematic panel-by-panel prompts written like screenplay directions\n- Switched to `--niji 7 --raw` (anime rendering) for all prompts\n- Art direction language: \"Gothic anime style, motion webtoon panel\"\n- Prompts specify camera angles (aerial shot, tracking shot, close-up, mid-shot, high-angle wide)\n- Emotional direction embedded in prompts (\"the face of someone who has confirmed a terrible suspicion\")\n- Explicit color theory (hex codes like `#1a1a2e`, \"warm emerald-gold palette fading to natural green\")\n- Aspect ratios: `--ar 16:9` for landscape panels, `--ar 9:16` for vertical webtoon format\n- Panel types labeled: FULL PAGE PANEL, establishing shot, texture study\n\n### Key Characters Illustrated\n\n| Character | Description | Frequency |\n|-----------|-------------|-----------|\n| **Gnome diviner/artificer** | Warm tan skin, golden-yellow spiked hair, gap-toothed grin, brass goggles, burgundy-red/gold mechanical armor. Also elderly bronze dragonborn cleric of Bahamut. | High (27+ prompts) |\n| **Elven paladin/archer** | Young blonde male elf, inherits blue dragon power. Ruby red rapier and sapphire shield. | Moderate |\n| **Tiefling warlock** | Blue/pink/scarlet variants. Mischievous, eldritch blast, lightning. | Moderate (9+ prompts) |\n| **Druid (female)** | Fiery red hair, shapeshifter (human to wolf mid-leap). \"Becomes an entire zoo's worth of animals.\" | Moderate (33+ prompts) |\n| **Half-ogres** | Two enormous companions, clubs over shoulders, \"confused as always.\" | Several |\n| **Cloaked human ranger** | Assassinates storm giant king, fights aboleth, dunks sapphire dragon egg. | Several |\n| **Reynaldine** | Fey Lord of Autumn, antler crown, autumn leaves cloak, raven black hair, sick with black ichor | Several |\n\n### Key Locations\n\n| volcano, phoenix overhead |\n| **Feywild courts** | Convergence of four seasons, sacrificial stone table, autumn/spring themes |\n\n### Campaign Moments Illustrated\n\nMajor story beats captured in the art:\n1. **The Heart of the Wild discovery** -- gnome reaches for massive emerald, face shows recognition/dread, not greed\n2. **Party fights Tiamat** -- adventurers vs five-headed dragon goddess ash\n5. **Drow backstory** -- child prince witnessing parents' disposal during rebellion\n6. **Creation myth tapestry** -- woman of fire, man of every element, prototypes leading to the next\n7. Bull/Taurus, Capricorn/Goatfish, Two-Headed Rabbit\n\n### Notable Prompts Showing Creative Vision\n\nThe March 2026 prompts are the most remarkable -- they read like screenplay directions. Example:\n\n> \"Extreme close-up of a gnome's face, lit from below by vivid emerald light. The gnome has warm tan skin, sharp features, intelligent eyes, and an immaculate dark suit collar visible at the bottom of frame. His expression is not what you expect from a gnome seeing treasure -- this is not greed, not excitement. This is recognition. Understanding. The face of someone who has confirmed a terrible suspicion and is afraid of what it means.\"\n\nThe DM's art direction evolved from simple `subject + modifiers` in 2022 to full cinematic compositions with emotional subtext, camera directions, color palettes, and narrative purpose in 2026. The March 2026 sprint appears to be the webcomic production pipeline referenced in the Mardov project docs -- systematically generating panels for what reads like an opening chapter: the party exploring the Forest Zenith, discovering the Heart of the Wild, and then the establishing shot of Argos where \"the story begins.\"\n\n### Technical Patterns\n\n- **Model**: Exclusively Midjourney. V4 (2022), V5 (2023), V6 (2024), Niji 7 (2026)\n- **Speed mode**: Mix of `(fast)` and `(relaxed)` -- relaxed mode used heavily in the 2026 sprint\n- **Aspect ratios**: 16:9 for landscape/cinematic panels, 9:16 for vertical webtoon panels, 1:1 for portraits\n- **Style references (`--sref`)**: Used sparingly (5 instances), suggesting the DM preferred natural Midjourney variation\n- **Iteration pattern**: Typically 2-5 generations per concept (initial + variations + upscales), with the 2026 sprint being more one-shot"}],"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1613,"cache_read_input_tokens":125835,"output_tokens":2366,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1613},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CZ9eQKfCLd8DnigSmur8N","type":"assistant","uuid":"08992a96-8164-46ed-ba39-5263f2ef0cb0","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:05:00.058Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}
